(Under the law of events, 2026-09-19: read as section 5.4's law of events says, there being no ray, thing, shadow or bit, no draw and no return; what this describes is an event, in transit or measured, handled by the Node's one computation, and a Node keeps nothing.)

**The phase circle stays small; a fine delay is a register, not a wide phase (2026-09-17, decided by the orchestrator on the model owner's delegation).** A family's phase is read only at a meeting, and only as a difference: the coherence table and the Born coupling read the difference of two phases, never a phase alone. So the width `phase_bits` is a choice of the family for the resolution its couplings need: eight steps resolve the Born table, and a wider circle is allowed but never required. The weakness of gravity does not live in the phase. The delay a field ray lays on the ray it meets is carried in a lag register with its own declared modulus, spent one Link toward the lagging side when it reaches that modulus, exactly as a heading is carried with a resolution of one part in 2^30 through six Ports; the modulus of the lag, not the phase circle, is the N of hypothesis 14, and it may be of any width because it enters no phase sum. The external body removed the need for a wide phase on the side of the source, since its mass is its amount (section 3.19); the lag register removes it on the side of the met ray. Consequences: the 75-bit phase of experiment A11 remains a check that the width leaks into no coupling, and nothing on the road to the confrontation runs needs it; the lag of feature 8, counted today in phase steps, is reworked to its own modulus as feature 8b, after feature 10; and the transition tables planned in the performance record keep one small phase dimension per meeting and carry the lag outside the key. (Under the law of events, 2026-09-19: read as section 5.4's law of events says, there being no ray, thing, shadow or bit, no draw and no return; what this describes is an event, in transit or measured, handled by the Node's one computation, and a Node keeps nothing.)

## 3.29 Observation is a frame-dependent representation of board reality

What an observer sees is a representation of the same underlying Node states and events, determined by the observer's reference frame, orientation, motion and measurement interaction. Observable records arise only from information that has causally reached the measuring system, not from an instantaneous view of the whole board.

```text
observed_description = Transform_frame(Readout(local_measurement_records))
```

Changing coordinates or basis changes an event's description; a measurement interaction may change state and record an outcome. These are distinct operations. Equivalent descriptions preserve declared invariants and consistent records. The Detector is a marked Node on the board (section 3.19), not an external device; it acts on what arrives through its Ports, on the board clock. A material eye or observer emerging on the board is a research goal, not a completed implementation. Detector, passive Recorder and Renderer remain distinct; a global audit display is not a physical observer.

## 3.30 One property engine, from abstract structure to detector experience

The purpose of Universe24 is to turn abstract mathematical descriptions of groups, representations, particle families and their properties into explicit board dynamics and, through a modeled detector, observable experience. Abstract does not mean imaginary. The engine uses one generic state schema and operation framework: fields, electrons, muons and gluons are distinguished by property content, representations and permitted interactions, not by separate fundamental engines. A ray is the propagation form of these properties, not an additional entity class. Uniform representation must retain physical differences, including charges, spin, statistics and couplings.

```text
property definitions + symmetry representations → Node state → propagation and interaction → detector records → observer-dependent display
```

At each Node, separately implemented generic operations evaluate owned properties and arrived inputs under immutable family/coupling definitions. The engine schedules, transports and validates; NodeState stores bounded data rather than physical formulas or executable expressions. Ordinary evolution is deterministic, while only a Node whose Detector bit is set draws (no draw since 2026-09-18, section 5.4, point 14). A Recorder stores evidence and a Renderer presents it. The material-eye and full species-dynamics goals remain separate from supported profiles; a visualization does not establish agreement with nature. (Under the law of events, 2026-09-19: read as section 5.4's law of events says, there being no ray, thing, shadow or bit, no draw and no return; what this describes is an event, in transit or measured, handled by the Node's one computation, and a Node keeps nothing.)

# 4. Central statement

Universe24 investigates one discrete Node-and-Link world in which properties propagate in ray form and matter, particles and mass are intended to emerge as stable patterns. Ordinary dynamics are local and deterministic; the only draw is at a Node whose Detector bit is set (no draw since 2026-09-18, section 5.4, point 14), and there is no shared resource, no hidden ordinary access and no second material world. Symmetry representations constrain generic property operations. Every mechanism must specify local state, arrivals, operations, owners, output clocks and causal transfers. The aim is to derive and test effective behavior without inserting the desired macroscopic laws. A defined contract or configured experiment is not yet a complete derived theory of nature. (Under the law of events, 2026-09-19: read as section 5.4's law of events says, there being no ray, thing, shadow or bit, no draw and no return; what this describes is an event, in transit or measured, handled by the Node's one computation, and a Node keeps nothing.)

# 5. Reading map

## 5.1 Node state and ray form

**State.** The state that moves is the ray (family properties, phase, heading, the number of steps it has made since its event, the information of its last event and, if that was a Detector event, its bit); a Node holds nothing but the rays resident this interval and, under a declared binding coupling, a bounded bound group. Since 2026-09-18 (section 5.4, definitions and points 22, 25): a ray carries an amount, a phase, a heading and a bit, a thing its momentum too; a shadow carries no step count (point 3); a Node is its six Ports, two lanes each, one real slot and one shadow slot per owner on every lane, with the parked shadows of point 22, and holds nothing else. Superseded on 2026-09-18 (the evening) by the law of the shadow, section 5.4: there is no bit and no real slot; every ray is a shadow, and a Node holds its six Ports, the parked shadows and, where it is matter, held content, which moves by its momentum (reading (v) there; the orchestrator's reading). (Under the law of events, 2026-09-19: read as section 5.4's law of events says, there being no ray, thing, shadow or bit, no draw and no return; what this describes is an event, in transit or measured, handled by the Node's one computation, and a Node keeps nothing.)

**Meetings.** At a meeting of rays the declared coupling between their families decides no interaction, a deterministic interaction with exact invariants and at most six events out, or a Detector interaction. There is no occupied channel and no capacity rule: rays cross, meet or bind by their declared couplings, and nothing is pushed back or made to wait for room. The occupied-channel displacement rule of the 2026-09-16 revision was deleted on 2026-09-17. Superseded for real rays on a lane on 2026-09-18 by point 25 of section 5.4 (model owner): a lane carries one real ray per interval, a thing steps into a lane only if the lane is free and otherwise keeps its heading and steps at the next Node, and two real rays of one family given one lane are one; nothing queues and nothing waits for room, the lane being a condition on the step and not a tie-break. Superseded on 2026-09-18 (the evening) by the law of the shadow, section 5.4: there is no real ray on a lane; a shadow that reaches held content is absorbed by the family's table, shadows of one number mix (point 24), and one real per lane and the merging of two reals are to be restated by round 8. (Under the law of events, 2026-09-19: read as section 5.4's law of events says, there being no ray, thing, shadow or bit, no draw and no return; what this describes is an event, in transit or measured, handled by the Node's one computation, and a Node keeps nothing.)

**Layers.** Event spacetime has layers: two events can happen at the same Node in the same interval in layers that do not communicate, because rays whose families have no declared coupling never meet; they cross as if the other were not there. A layer is a set of families that couple; a meeting exists only inside a layer.

## 5.2 Generic operations at a Node

Arrived input, one bounded local update per tick, a local proposal and atomic commit. Physical execution is at the Node; the separate generic operations package is code organization, not another physical place. Immutable family and coupling definitions own physical transformations and conserved readouts. The engine schedules, transports and validates, while NodeState contains bounded data and ownership, not physical formulas.

**The Node's law in five steps (model owner, 2026-09-17).** A Node knows nothing about electrons or stars; it is a switchboard with six Ports, six output clocks and a table to read. Each interval: (1) receive what arrived on the six Ports, with what is resident (a bound group); (2) apply its mark, if any: a Detector draws once per arrival, 0 returned and 1 continued; an external body absorbs into its sink and radiates by its amount; (3) meet by table, layer by layer: families with no declared coupling cross as if alone, families with one produce the outputs the table says, with exact invariants and the remainder placed by section 3.17; (4) stamp every output as a new event ray, with its event's Ports, shares and steps 0; (5) depart, each ray through its Port when that face's clock is ready. The two marks, the Detector and the external body, are the whole apparatus of a world: they are the only places where the board does something the tables do not say, and both are declarations in the initial file, never physics. Since 2026-09-18 (section 5.4): a Node has no output clocks and no departure waits for one (point 21), the one wait being the tick of point 23; a mark draws nothing, it absorbs a thing and returns a shadow (points 6, 14); a shadow meeting a thing is a push and a return (point 3), the shadows of one owner are mixed among the six Ports (point 24), and the tables of step (3) stand for a thing meeting a thing. Superseded on 2026-09-18 (the evening) by the law of the shadow, section 5.4: no thing meets a thing and nothing is returned; each interval the Node mixes the shadows that arrived (point 24), and where it holds content it absorbs the whole quanta by the family's table, holds or releases again, and releases in proportion to its content, the step of held content being a release toward the neighbour and an absorption there (readings (i) and (v) there). (Under the law of events, 2026-09-19: read as section 5.4's law of events says, there being no ray, thing, shadow or bit, no draw and no return; what this describes is an event, in transit or measured, handled by the Node's one computation, and a Node keeps nothing.)

## 5.3 Couplings, fields and timing

Propagation, funded emission, remainders, six independent output clocks, no input delay and fixed neighboring Link transit H. Mass representation, rest phase and computation delay are distinct. A free ray never meets its own field because the field is faster and leaves ahead of it or away from it (section 3.5); newly output-delayed moving emitters remain unsupported until their composition is defined and tested. The strong-interaction long-residence and computation-field-emission idea is a research hypothesis, not proof of nuclear binding; weak conversion channels need their own explicit operators. Both, and polarization, are catalog entries on the same engine (section 3.26), scheduled after feature 10. The six output clocks and the computation delay are superseded on 2026-09-18 by section 5.4, points 21 and 23: every ray moves one Link per interval, and the one wait is the tick a thing pays per whole quantum it reads. Superseded again on 2026-09-18 (the evening) by the law of the shadow, section 5.4: the one wait reads the size and is paid by every quantum, held or in flight (reading (iii) there); the emission is held content's, every interval in proportion to its content, and whether its gravity and electricity are free and its light paid is what round 8 must show. (Under the law of events, 2026-09-19: read as section 5.4's law of events says, there being no ray, thing, shadow or bit, no draw and no return; what this describes is an event, in transit or measured, handled by the Node's one computation, and a Node keeps nothing.)

## 5.4 The Detector

The law in force is the Beam Law, `beam-v1`, decided on 2026-09-19 (the law of the ray; its published design and implementation contract is [BEAM_LAW.md](BEAM_LAW.md)); with `amplitude-v1` (decided 2026-09-20; [its design](designs/amplitude-v1/DESIGN.md)) the law of every world with a lamp since the one click of the same day, the world key `amplitude` deleted then ([record 135](LOG_2026-09-20.md#135-stage-vii-step-4-built-on-the-branch-the-one-click-the-record-form-the-law-every-lamp-births-records-the-key-amplitude-deleted-the-crowd-threshold-deleted-the-8-lamp-free-gate-worlds-byte-identical-to-main-the-7-lamp-worlds-changed-by-the-record-form-itself-the-stop-rule-read-as-the-crowd-threshold-series--every-lamp-world-the-k-table-unchanged-the-share-worlds-integers-as-expected-w27_beams-host-cost-7-gb-not-completed-the-gate-review-running), [record 145](LOG_2026-09-20.md#145-the-one-click-landed-pr-395-main-e4b649a0-stage-vii-step-4-of-amplitude-v1-under-record-137-1-and-2-every-lamp-births-records-one-click-per-record-by-the-ladder-u-the-records-own-field-the-push-by-share-the-key-amplitude-deleted-as-built-the-gate-reviews-three-items-closed-b-the-layer-releases-a-gathered-records-offers-bit-identical-on-14-of-14-completed-gate-worlds-and-the-four-k-worlds-w27_beams-memory-bounded-at-33-gb-but-its-time-open-c-a1s-verdict-from-the-gathers-the-ladder-lands-five-u-alone-on-the-screen-no-record-visibility-formed-the-gate-set-of-sixteen-all-and-only-the-lamp-worlds-changed-b2-re-run-under-the-one-click-with-its-binding-pins-unchanged-the-fraction-free-unification-started-from-its-head-the-architects-pr-3-and-phase-2-signalled); [BEAM_LAW note 37 (x)](BEAM_LAW.md#10-implementation-notes-2026-09-19-the-implementation)). This section keeps the model owner's decisions, one line each, in the owner's words where he gave them; the record behind each line, with the readings, findings, proposals and runs of that day, is in the dated logs ([2026-09-17 and 2026-09-18](LOG_2026-09-18.md), [2026-09-19](LOG_2026-09-19.md), [2026-09-20](LOG_2026-09-20.md)). A record is never edited; a decision that a later decision supersedes stays here with "superseded by". The records were moved out of this section on 2026-09-20 by the owner's decision of that day ([record 87](LOG_2026-09-20.md#87-decided-the-trimming-pull-request-after-amplitude-v1-lands)).

- **The crowd audit** (the model owner, 2026-09-21, "are you checking the crowd everywhere? I think this is something very, very important for us"): one table of the law, [BEAM_LAW note 47](BEAM_LAW.md#10-implementation-notes-2026-09-19-the-implementation), a row per rule in force with what it reads of its own record and what of the crowd (the one reading set: everything at the Node but the reader's own number, and the six neighbours); 13 rules read the crowd and 10 their own record (the walk, the escape at a face, the click's choice, the re-emission, the rotation, the turn, the birth, the release, the border `lifetime`, the decay's timing); whether the crowd enters the birth or the turn is the owner's call -- [record - **The GameBoard is reached only through detectors (the model owner, 2026-09-21):** in the owner's words, "in our world there is no way to reach the world of the GameBoard; we know how to reach it only through detectors, and the detectors are probably limited": every value compared with nature is a detector's reading (a detector reads the moments of the arriving rows at its Node and one threshold per record, never the state vector, never a row's phase directly; 5.7, records 196, 205, 210); every role states it so. Recorded beside it, EXPLICITLY AS THE OWNER'S UNPROVEN HYPOTHESIS and not as a rule or a decision: "a detector is life, something that someone with life made" ([record 277](LOG_2026-09-20.md#277-the-owners-line-for-the-highlights-in-our-world-there-is-no-way-to-reach-the-world-of-the-gameboard-we-reach-it-only-through-detectors-and-the-detectors-are-probably-limited-and-his-stated-unproven-assumption-that-a-detector-is-life-something-made-by-someone-with-life-the-owner-2026-09-21-about-0555z-to-the-derivation-mathematician-relayed-to-the-boss-translated-make-sure-that-this-line-of-mine-enters-the-highlights-very-clearly-and-that-everyone-knows-it-in-our-world-there-is-no-way-to-reach-the-world-of-the-gameboard-we-know-how-to-reach-it-only-through-detectors-and-the-detectors-are-probably-limited-and-one-of-my-assumptions-is-that-a-detector-is-life-in-fact-something-that-someone-with-life-made-but-that-is-entirely-a-hypothesis-it-is-not-proven-here-and-we-have-not-discussed-it-it-is-only-an-assumption-1-the-line-enters-highlights-54-as-one-entry-visible-to-every-role-in-the-owners-words-with-the-laws-statement-of-the-limit-beside-it-a-detector-reads-only-the-moments-of-the-arriving-rows-at-its-node-order-0-1-2-and-the-age-and-one-threshold-per-record-the-click-so-it-reads-content-and-never-the-state-vector-never-a-metric-never-a-rows-phase-directly-records-196-205-57s-nobody-sees-the-state-record-210-171-and-195-a-mass-is-never-clicked-only-read-four-ways-the-limits-are-the-reading-operators-stated-not-assumed-2-the-assumption-a-detector-is-life-is-recorded-here-and-in-the-same-54-entry-explicitly-as-the-owners-unproven-hypothesis-not-a-rule-not-a-decision-not-discussed-nothing-in-the-law-the-derivations-or-the-paper-depends-on-it-and-no-role-reads-it-as-one-the-papers-part-i-says-the-first-part-where-it-names-the-gameboard-record-275-2)).

results: the GameBoard periodic by declaration (the boundary key; a convention, the physical question open per record 270 (3) and DERIVATIONS_BEAM section 20) and the growing wall under expansion-v1 with H (the Hubble rate) declared, absent by default (stated in DERIVATIONS_BEAM section 20.4, not built); the law derives nothing on dark energy or dark matter: the coasting expansion q = 0 is - **A click is the passage of information from Node to Node, at most one Node per interval; its content an amplitude with phase that splits between staying and hopping; reality is the clicks; Lorentz is the symmetry of the clicks, assumed of the world and required of the board** (the model owner, 2026-09-22, "go for it; - **The paper's basis: why physics behaves like modern algebra, and when the transition from ordinary physics to the algebra was made** (the model owner, 2026-09-22: "what we must explain in the paper is how we got from ordinary physics to algebraic mathematics, when we made that transition; it is in main and in the code; from the beginning of the paper to the end, one only needs to show why physics behaves like modern algebra, otherwise one is just showing formulas in modern algebra; with the couplings and everything we did; it must be shown in the paper"): the argument in order: the measured quantities are counts at clicks and their ratios, so the Outside is arithmetic in Q; the law Inside is integer and local (the six verbs, the three tests of every rule); the couplings are tables and the Born rule a coupling computed from N (point 17); the structure-preserving maps between click families are the Lorentz group up to scale (the click theorem); so every result is an identity of the algebra checked against the register's readings by kind, never a formula fitted; the dated transitions are written from main and the code with their records and readings (docs/designs/algebra_transition, in progress); [record - **Every phenomenon is written Outside, down to Inside, and back Outside; interference is shown by formulas alone** (the model owner, 2026-09-22, to the paper's writer: "interference, one slit, two slits, Mach-Zehnder, can be shown in the paper with formulas alone, what passes and what does not; the pattern of every phenomenon is Outside, the measurement, down to Inside, the rows, the phases, the merge, the evaluation, and back Outside, the click's counts against the reading, because every measurement is Outside but the computation must enter Inside"): the Outside quantity is named first with its detector and place, then the Inside step that computes it, then the conversion back to the click's counts and the register's reading by kind; the two slits, one slit and Mach-Zehnder are worked in the paper's own formulas (the merge's cancel, the offer proportional to 1 + cos Delta, the rungs, the counts over N births), the register's readings the confirmation; [record 795](LOG_2026-09-20.md#795-the-owner-through-the-writer-interference-by-formulas-alone-the-pattern-of-every-phenomenon-is-outside-down-to-inside-and-back-outside-2026-09-22-0556z-recorded-by-the-boss-at-0557z-paper-writer-relays-the-owners-word-given-in-his-session-for-the-boss-too-interference-one-slit-two-slits-mach-zehnder-can-be-shown-in-the-paper-with-formulas-alone-what-passes-and-what-does-not-the-pattern-of-every-phenomenon-is-outside-the-measurement-down-to-inside-the-rows-the-phases-the-merge-the-evaluation-and-back-outside-the-clicks-counts-against-the-reading-because-every-measurement-is-outside-but-the-computation-must-enter-inside-folded-by-the-writer-into-planmd-as-section-8c-of-the-reshaping-plan-at-fdc91cb8b993b6cd7d5688b31eac5ffe98d25a71-claudepaper-owner-review-five-pushed-nothing-applied-the-two-slits-in-the-papers-own-formulas-two-rows-of-one-record-m--2-the-phases-per-link-the-merge-with-the-cancel-at-delta--n2-the-offer-rx--512-cp_1--cp_22--sp_1--sp_22-proportional-to-1--cos-delta-the-rungs-the-count-b_x---b_x-1-over-n-births-the-dark-pixels-rung-of-width-0-is-does-not-pass-one-slit-no-pair-nothing-cancels-flat-within-the-fans-grain-mach-zehnder-the-two-phasors-adding-at-one-port-and-cancelling-at-the-other-the-tables-16811682-and-11682-the-clicks-640-then-the-pair-malus-ghz-and-the-clock-in-a-crowd-by-the-same-three-lines-each-the-registers-readings-become-the-confirmation-table-2s-rows-the-pointer-the-visibility-clause-kdeltak0-restored-in-section-6-about-twenty-lines-for-the-four-worked-phenomena-in-place-of-table-2s-seven-rows-and-section-6s-l2b-narrative-net-about-eight-it-rides-the-reshaping-commit-with-the-owners-go-and-the-shas-of-parts-6-to-9-the-boss-relayed-the-pattern-to-the-light-mathematician-with-the-writers-two-slit-chain-to-verify-against-the-engine-and-the-register-in-the-three-formula-shape-trig_01xivzuf843knplnjmekoyxj-and-to-the-einstein-mathematician-trig_01yjpyhjnmt1slyns8ctka8c-the-pattern-added-to-highlights-54-as-the-owners-decision).

- **Locality Outside too: a packet passes from place to place and never jumps; modern algebra and limits first, a run only after** (the model owner, 2026-09-22: "velocity in the real world is passing a click with information to the neighbouring Node and receiving that packet there; the passage is always Outside to Inside, moving, and coming out again; the packet passes from place to place, it cannot jump, a kind of locality also Outside; everything that can be shown in modern algebra is shown in modern algebra, with limits, and only after that a run if needed; a run of clicks in the world above is the same run, one runs clicks"): the Outside's locality is the click theorem's A1 read from above and is stated as an assumption beside A1 and A2 in every derivation and in the paper; the Outside inherits the Inside's locality through the conversion; the rule of algebra first is in the Boss's skill and in skills/workflow.md (record 762); a run Outside reads clicks alone under declared conditions; [record 799](LOG_2026-09-20.md#799-the-owner-locality-outside-too-a-packet-passes-from-place-to-place-and-never-jumps-modern-algebra-and-limits-first-a-run-only-after-a-run-outside-is-a-run-of-clicks-2026-09-22-0605z-recorded-by-the-boss-at-0601z-the-owners-words-by-voice-then-one-can-also-compare-a-clock-but-one-must-also-put-our-assumption-that-velocity-in-the-real-world-is-passing-a-click-with-information-to-the-neighbouring-node-and-receiving-that-packet-at-the-neighbouring-node-having-in-effect-moved-to-the-neighbouring-node-the-passage-is-always-outside-to-inside-moving-to-wherever-one-wants-and-then-one-must-come-out-of-there-but-in-the-real-world-what-we-say-is-that-the-packet-passes-from-place-to-place-it-cannot-jump-one-can-call-it-a-kind-of-locality-also-outside-and-set-yourself-a-law-in-your-skill-everything-that-can-be-shown-in-formulas-in-modern-algebra-is-shown-in-modern-algebra-always-the-preference-to-modern-algebra-and-limits-and-so-on-and-only-after-that-a-run-if-needed-and-if-one-wants-a-run-of-clicks-in-the-world-above-it-is-exactly-the-same-thing-there-are-conditions-there-the-same-runs-but-one-runs-clicks-the-bosss-answer-the-outsides-locality-is-a1-read-from-above-velocity-outside-is-a-click-carrying-information-to-the-neighbouring-node-a-packet-passes-from-place-to-place-and-never-jumps-every-passage-goes-outside-to-inside-moves-there-and-comes-out-again-so-the-outside-inherits-the-insides-locality-through-the-conversion-this-is-stated-as-an-assumption-in-every-derivation-order-and-in-the-papers-assumptions-beside-a1-and-a2-the-rule-written-into-skillsboss-orchestratorskillmd-as-a-subsection-of-a-formula-gives-a-run-proves-modern-algebra-and-limits-first-a-run-only-after-if-needed-a-run-outside-the-same-kind-of-run-with-its-declared-conditions-the-reading-the-clicks-alone-skillsworkflowmds-refinement-of-record-762-stands-as-the-shared-rule-sent-as-one-clause-to-the-derivation-mathematician-trig_01ait4tfmyechguxncjmccyv-the-light-mathematician-trig_01nikarhy9xcfzf3eyknmh4b-and-the-einstein-mathematician-trig_01kxge4h8r1d4opa3zqtkyeo-added-to-highlights-54-as-the-owners-decision).

### Decisions of 2026-09-20

- **`wave` is the default reading of a detector, `beam` a declared option**; Bell must read S = 2 unchanged under it — [record 02](LOG_2026-09-20.md#02-decided-wave-is-the-default-reading-of-a-detector-beam-a-declared-option); supersedes [record 24 of 2026-09-19](LOG_2026-09-19.md#24-decided-the-detector-key-reading-beam-by-default-the-pairing-rule-and-wave-the-coherent-sum-an-option) (`beam` by default); under `amplitude-v1`, `sum` is the reading of the one pointer over a record, `wave` over the crowd, one scope key (unification 4, record 86).
- **The principle**: "on the game board a ray, and in the world a wave is received", the standing statement of the model — [record 03](LOG_2026-09-20.md#03-the-principle-on-the-gameboard-a-ray-in-the-world-a-wave); read since the evening as a statement about a crowd: the sum the click reads is over the rows of one record, and the wave is the form the list of clicks draws ([record 77](LOG_2026-09-20.md#77-the-corrected-sentence-without-a-caveat-and-the-wave)).
- **The name of the board is GameBoard** ("that is what it is to be called in English in every place, also in the code: GameBoard") — [record 05](LOG_2026-09-20.md#05-decided-the-name-of-the-board-is-gameboard); landed with the names, record 44.
- **Series G, the Hubble diagram behind the detector** ("let it check whether what is observed today is also seen in our detector") — [record 10](LOG_2026-09-20.md#10-decided-series-g-the-hubble-diagram-behind-the-detector); landed, record 32; its "surprise" is not a finding, record 60.
- **Every experiment shows its results on a page with the GameBoard drawn**, why it was tested and what the conclusion is — [record 12](LOG_2026-09-20.md#12-decided-every-experiment-shows-its-results-on-a-page-with-the-gameboard-drawn).
- **The GameBoard is deterministic; the detector's world is not**, by the uncertainty principle in the detector's world — [record 15](LOG_2026-09-20.md#15-decided-the-gameboard-is-deterministic-the-detectors-world-is-not-by-the-uncertainty-in-the-detectors-world).
- **Two kinds of readings**: a detector reading, the only kind reality has, and a GameBoard reading, the host's view ("In reality there is no such thing"); a comparison with nature uses detector readings only — [record 20](LOG_2026-09-20.md#20-decided-two-kinds-of-readings-in-reality-there-is-no-such-thing).
- **The experimenter's skill**: every real experiment measures behind a detector or at an external thing; "the GameBoard is not measurable by a human" — [record 22](LOG_2026-09-20.md#22-decided-the-experimenters-skill-the-gameboard-is-not-measurable-by-a-human).
- **The strong force's range is a lifetime, L** ("excellent, go for it"): a family key counted on the event's own age; the event whose age reaches L makes no next event but an escape click in the ledger — [record 23](LOG_2026-09-20.md#23-decided-the-strong-forces-range-is-a-lifetime-l); the strong force landed as series I, record 61.
- **The weak force is arranged in the world's terms, and the law is asked what else it predicts** ("Arrange the weak force according to our world, and check whether we predict more things") — [record 25](LOG_2026-09-20.md#25-decided-the-weak-force-is-arranged-in-the-worlds-terms-and-the-law-is-asked-what-else-it-predicts); the physicist's design, record 36; landed as `weak-v1`, record 78.
- **"Our laws are on the GameBoard; in the detector one sees other laws"**: a comparison with nature is always between detector readings and nature's laws, never between the GameBoard's rules and nature's laws — [record 26](LOG_2026-09-20.md#26-decided-our-laws-are-on-the-gameboard-in-the-detector-one-sees-other-laws).
- **One mechanism for all the laws on the GameBoard** ("maybe one mechanism for all the laws on the board?"; "just check that it is correct and that it works"): the one coupling a signed inner product over the columns a family declares per unit of content, gravity the column every family has — [record 27](LOG_2026-09-20.md#27-decided-one-mechanism-for-all-the-laws-on-the-gameboard); the mathematician's verdict on the columns, record 35.
- **The catalog of the entities**: every entity physics knows is one row of the law's keys, the external things placeable on the GameBoard — [record 30](LOG_2026-09-20.md#30-decided-the-catalog-of-the-entities); landed, record 45.
- **The contact rule is the generic one, through the table; the strong force goes to implementation** ("go") — [record 34](LOG_2026-09-20.md#34-decided-the-contact-rule-generic-through-the-table-the-strong-force-goes-to-implementation).
- **The law of the ray is the Beam Law** (`beam-v1`, `"law": "beam"`; "ray" stays the informal name of the beam) — [record 37](LOG_2026-09-20.md#37-decided-the-law-of-the-ray-is-the-beam-law); on the GameBoard there are only events, `NatureBeam` the record of an event in transit ([record 21](LOG_2026-09-20.md#21-and-there-is-no-ray-there-is-only-an-event), "there is no ray, there is only an event"); the names landed, record 44.
- **"Go on everything; just make sure again that it is good and generic"**: the weak force in the recommended order (`phase_width`, then `become` with `weak-v1`, then the W world), D-1, the moving body's clock as the engine has it, series K and A10 at a low rate after series I; the physics-rule reviewer on every changed rule as the closing gate — [record 38](LOG_2026-09-20.md#38-decided-go-on-everything-just-make-sure-again-that-it-is-good-and-generic); the two closing gates, records 70 and 73.
- **Issue #359, step A yes, step B no**: under `wave` the threshold reads the square of the coherent pointer; a pointer kept between intervals is refused ("B is a detector parameter that is not known to work in the real world") — [record 40](LOG_2026-09-20.md#40-decided-issue-359-step-a-yes-step-b-no).; superseded on step A by the one click of the same day ([record 135](LOG_2026-09-20.md#135-stage-vii-step-4-built-on-the-branch-the-one-click-the-record-form-the-law-every-lamp-births-records-the-key-amplitude-deleted-the-crowd-threshold-deleted-the-8-lamp-free-gate-worlds-byte-identical-to-main-the-7-lamp-worlds-changed-by-the-record-form-itself-the-stop-rule-read-as-the-crowd-threshold-series--every-lamp-world-the-k-table-unchanged-the-share-worlds-integers-as-expected-w27_beams-host-cost-7-gb-not-completed-the-gate-review-running), [record 145](LOG_2026-09-20.md#145-the-one-click-landed-pr-395-main-e4b649a0-stage-vii-step-4-of-amplitude-v1-under-record-137-1-and-2-every-lamp-births-records-one-click-per-record-by-the-ladder-u-the-records-own-field-the-push-by-share-the-key-amplitude-deleted-as-built-the-gate-reviews-three-items-closed-b-the-layer-releases-a-gathered-records-offers-bit-identical-on-14-of-14-completed-gate-worlds-and-the-four-k-worlds-w27_beams-memory-bounded-at-33-gb-but-its-time-open-c-a1s-verdict-from-the-gathers-the-ladder-lands-five-u-alone-on-the-screen-no-record-visibility-formed-the-gate-set-of-sixteen-all-and-only-the-lamp-worlds-changed-b2-re-run-under-the-one-click-with-its-binding-pins-unchanged-the-fraction-free-unification-started-from-its-head-the-architects-pr-3-and-phase-2-signalled); [MIGRATION (vii-4)](MIGRATION.md#the-amplitude-law-on-2026-09-20-vii-4-the-one-click-the-record-form-the-law)): the threshold is the amount summed over the set under both readings, the pointer giving the set's phase and its record.
- **Every external thing brought from the real world into the GameBoard carries its uncertainty like the detector; on the GameBoard it is certain** — [record 41](LOG_2026-09-20.md#41-decided-every-external-thing-brought-into-the-gameboard-carries-its-uncertainty-like-the-detector). With "there is no outside" ([record 58](LOG_2026-09-20.md#58-there-is-no-outside)): a detector's Nodes are on the GameBoard; its reading, the layer and the click, is the external thing's, where the uncertainty and the one-way border already were ([record 74](LOG_2026-09-20.md#74-the-principles-of-amplitude-v1-in-ten-sentences), the note).
- **A mathematician on the formulas and the genericity** — [record 42](LOG_2026-09-20.md#42-decided-a-mathematician-on-the-formulas-and-the-genericity); the verdict, the Beam Law two formulas and one table composed, record 48.
- **The four unifications of the formulas, after the strong force lands** ("go on all four after the strong one") — [record 49](LOG_2026-09-20.md#49-decided-the-four-unifications-of-the-formulas-after-the-strong-force-lands); landed, record 64.
- **The generalized collision: an event in transit reads the crowd reversibly** ("go for it") — [record 51](LOG_2026-09-20.md#51-decided-the-generalized-collision-an-event-in-transit-reads-the-crowd-reversibly); its design and the pilot's result, record 62; the pilot route closed by its own result (record 63).
- **The API of the GameBoard is an emitter in and a detector out** ("when you intervene in the GameBoard it requires a detector on the GameBoard or an emitter; that is the API") — [record 53](LOG_2026-09-20.md#53-decided-the-api-of-the-gameboard-is-an-emitter-in-and-a-detector-out).
- **#362 waits for the pilot** ("waiting for the pilot; update me when there is a result") — [record 54](LOG_2026-09-20.md#54-decided-362-waits-for-the-pilot); superseded by record 63 (the pilot route closed) and by record 72.
- **The amplitude, option 1 now** ("now 1"): an event is a number at one place with a phase; the single-click build-up and Bell's 2.83 the declared and measured limit of the law — [record 63](LOG_2026-09-20.md#63-decided-the-amplitude-option-1-now); superseded by record 72: without the key `amplitude` the GameBoard is this state (an event a number at one place, S = 2, every registered world byte-identical); under the key, `amplitude-v1`.
- **WITHDRAWN: the frozen clock** as a route to the pair's correlation ("OK, the clock freeze is cancelled"); option 1 stood as decided — [record 65](LOG_2026-09-20.md#65-withdrawn-the-frozen-clock-as-a-route-to-the-pairs-correlation).
- **#363 runs now** ("go for it"): Bell with the choosers on the GameBoard — [record 66](LOG_2026-09-20.md#66-decided-363-runs-now); measured, record 71 (S = 2 exactly, every marginal 1/2); landed, record 75.
- **The meeting, M-R** ("go for it if it seems generic and smart to you"): an event in transit reads the crowd as a body does, a report, not a balance — [record 67](LOG_2026-09-20.md#67-decided-the-meeting-m-r); landed, record 75.
- **"Locality must not be harmed. The GameBoard is the future that could be; the click chooses one"**: the GameBoard computes every branch locally and exactly; absorption at a detector is an offer; the world is a reading in which u, the birth phase, chooses one — [record 72](LOG_2026-09-20.md#72-decided-locality-must-not-be-harmed-the-gameboard-is-the-future-that-could-be-the-click-chooses-one); supersedes the amplitude decision of the morning (record 63; option 3 as a keyed alternative): under the world key `amplitude` the law of records, branches and the click reading one record's sum is the model, `amplitude-v1`, absent by default; the key deleted at the one click, every lamp a record ([record 135](LOG_2026-09-20.md#135-stage-vii-step-4-built-on-the-branch-the-one-click-the-record-form-the-law-every-lamp-births-records-the-key-amplitude-deleted-the-crowd-threshold-deleted-the-8-lamp-free-gate-worlds-byte-identical-to-main-the-7-lamp-worlds-changed-by-the-record-form-itself-the-stop-rule-read-as-the-crowd-threshold-series--every-lamp-world-the-k-table-unchanged-the-share-worlds-integers-as-expected-w27_beams-host-cost-7-gb-not-completed-the-gate-review-running), [record 145](LOG_2026-09-20.md#145-the-one-click-landed-pr-395-main-e4b649a0-stage-vii-step-4-of-amplitude-v1-under-record-137-1-and-2-every-lamp-births-records-one-click-per-record-by-the-ladder-u-the-records-own-field-the-push-by-share-the-key-amplitude-deleted-as-built-the-gate-reviews-three-items-closed-b-the-layer-releases-a-gathered-records-offers-bit-identical-on-14-of-14-completed-gate-worlds-and-the-four-k-worlds-w27_beams-memory-bounded-at-33-gb-but-its-time-open-c-a1s-verdict-from-the-gathers-the-ladder-lands-five-u-alone-on-the-screen-no-record-visibility-formed-the-gate-set-of-sixteen-all-and-only-the-lamp-worlds-changed-b2-re-run-under-the-one-click-with-its-binding-pins-unchanged-the-fraction-free-unification-started-from-its-head-the-architects-pr-3-and-phase-2-signalled)).
- **The principles of `amplitude-v1` in ten sentences**, the standing form — [record 74](LOG_2026-09-20.md#74-the-principles-of-amplitude-v1-in-ten-sentences). The layer: the detectors' shared record is the apparatus's, at the one-way place, the only non-local operation; the offline reading of the record is a tool, not the law (superseding the orchestrator's reading of record 72 on the layer).
- **What may hold state**: a Node holds nothing beyond the events there and the GameBoard is O(1) per Node; the apparatus's layer, at the one-way place, holds the live records' tables (the host pays 2^n); a hub adjacent to every Node is refused — [record 74](LOG_2026-09-20.md#74-the-principles-of-amplitude-v1-in-ten-sentences) (principles 5 and 9) with [record 69](LOG_2026-09-20.md#69-recorded-365-the-amplitude-as-a-hub) (#365, the hub refused as the register under a new name).
- **The corrected sentence, without a caveat, and the wave**: "The world is the list of clicks. The GameBoard computes every possible future between click and click, and the next click chooses and adds a row to the list. Classical and quantum are the same GameBoard at a different density of clicks. One law, one measurement, one world." On the GameBoard rows, numbers, paths; at the click the detector reads the sum of one record's rows, squared, and u chooses; in the world the histogram of the clicks is the wave — [record 77](LOG_2026-09-20.md#77-the-corrected-sentence-without-a-caveat-and-the-wave).
- **The click defined by what it does**: the one operation that changes the possibilities, removing combinations from a record and adding one row to the world's list; everything else on the GameBoard is invertible — [record 80](LOG_2026-09-20.md#80-the-click-defined-by-what-it-does).
- **One quantum, from the birth to the click**, the standing form of principle 4 in ten points — [record 82](LOG_2026-09-20.md#82-one-quantum-from-the-birth-to-the-click).
- **The gate that ties two records, added to the design** ("add the gate that ties two records to the design"); CNOT the reference gate — [record 83](LOG_2026-09-20.md#83-the-gate-that-ties-two-records-added-to-the-design).

### Decisions of 2026-09-19

- **One speed**: "A quantum passes, like everything, at the speed of light between Nodes"; what we see are events, and only through a Detector — [record 01](LOG_2026-09-19.md#01-one-speed-and-what-is-seen).
- **The law of events: no fields, no matter, no registers; only events** — [record 02](LOG_2026-09-19.md#02-the-law-of-events-no-fields-no-matter-no-registers-only-events); superseded the same day by the law of the ray (record 15).
- **"I approve the proposal"**: a release costs the emitter by its phase rate, E = h f with h the declared `quantum`; the two gaps recorded — [record 10](LOG_2026-09-19.md#10-i-approve-the-proposal-a-release-costs-the-emitter-e--h-f-the-two-gaps).
- **The law of the ray** ("Go with what we talked about, fast; there is no wave in the Node", record 14): the Node holds no wave; a unit is a ray with a record on the digital line of its momentum at one speed; the collision a bijection; the click the only one-way border; an open face is a detector — [record 15](LOG_2026-09-19.md#15-decided-the-law-of-the-ray); named the Beam Law on 2026-09-20 (record 37 of that day). The GameBoard of a run is open on every face (what leaves is a click on the face detector), an axis may be periodic, a closed one is refused; the closed board of 2026-09-18 ([record 20](LOG_2026-09-18.md#20-the-board-of-a-run-is-closed-and-k-is-a-constant)) is superseded.
- **"I approve 1 and 3, and 2 with the physicist"**: the tables generated from the keys and a world declares only what differs; the one reading function the amount-weighted moments of order 0, 1 and 2 of the direction vectors; the push as one bilinear form, with the physics-rule review — [record 18](LOG_2026-09-19.md#18-i-approve-1-and-3-and-2-with-the-physicist).
- **"Try; check what code can be cleaned; the ray is NatureBeam everywhere; make sure there is optimization in everything"** — [record 19](LOG_2026-09-19.md#19-try-check-what-code-can-be-cleaned-the-ray-is-naturebeam-everywhere).
- **The detector key `reading`**, `beam` by default and `wave` the coherent sum, an option — [record 24](LOG_2026-09-19.md#24-decided-the-detector-key-reading-beam-by-default-the-pairing-rule-and-wave-the-coherent-sum-an-option); superseded by [record 02 of 2026-09-20](LOG_2026-09-20.md#02-decided-wave-is-the-default-reading-of-a-detector-beam-a-declared-option) (`wave` the default, `beam` a declared option).
- **`charge` is the charge per unit of content of a family**, an integer or a pair [n, d]; a measured event's charge is that times its content; the push one product — [record 26](LOG_2026-09-19.md#26-decided-charge-is-the-charge-per-unit-of-content-of-a-family-the-push-one-product).
- **Detector as physical Nodes**: "The detector is itself Nodes"; "The important thing is the detector sensitivity" — [record 28](LOG_2026-09-19.md#28-detector-as-physical-nodes-cell-sensitivity-and-information).
- **Separate entity definitions**: "The detector settings are in an entity definitions file, not in the engine" — [record 29](LOG_2026-09-19.md#29-separate-entity-definitions).

### Decisions of 2026-09-17 and 2026-09-18 (superseded on 2026-09-19 by the law of the ray)

- **The Detector's bit is a property of the ray** (2026-09-17) — [record 03](LOG_2026-09-18.md#03-the-detectors-bit-is-a-property-of-the-ray); superseded by the law of the bit the next day.
- **A click is an absorption** (2026-09-18) — [record 04](LOG_2026-09-18.md#04-a-click-is-an-absorption).
- **The law of the bit: the thing or its shadow**, its definitions and its points 15 to 25, the five rules settled, "a thing does not emit" then "a thing emits" — [records 05 to 26](LOG_2026-09-18.md#07-the-law-of-the-bit-the-thing-or-its-shadow); superseded the same evening by the law of the shadow.
- **The law of the shadow: only shadows and events** (2026-09-18, the evening) — [record 29](LOG_2026-09-18.md#29-the-law-of-the-shadow-only-shadows-and-events); superseded on 2026-09-19 by the law of events, then by the law of the ray.
- **The board of a run is closed, and K is a constant** (2026-09-18) — [record 20](LOG_2026-09-18.md#20-the-board-of-a-run-is-closed-and-k-is-a-constant); the GameBoard of a run is open since the Beam Law (a closed one is refused).

**How to add a record.** A record of a day (a reading, a finding, a run, a proposal, a question, the owner's words) is appended to `docs/LOG_<date>.md` under the next `### NN.` heading, never to this section; only a decision of the model owner is added here, as one line under its date with a link to its record. A number is never reused or moved, and a record is never edited.


## 5.5 Acceptance tests and open decisions

Every supported operation needs declared initial ownership, input, operator, expected output and model time, conserved readouts, edge cases and failure conditions. Accepted principles are not reopened by missing code or tests. Numerical family rules and Detector distributions need explicit closure where still undefined. The once-only encounter requirement needs independent tick traces and implementation evidence. A stated acceptance requirement is not a passing test.

**How tests are written and run (model owner, 2026-09-17).** The engine is generic and its rules are separated, so a test exercises one generic rule in isolation on a minimal board and nothing else: one test module per rule, one per feature of the ray-event model, with the expected integers written down before the first run. No test pins the numbers of an example world, compares two worlds or reproduces a known experiment; those are research runs (section 6), made once, recorded with a fingerprint and a date, and never repeated as tests. A change is checked only against the tests that depend on what it changed, selected by the import graph; the whole suite runs together only when the shared core changes (the Node and its Ports, the order of the cycle, the bounded integers, the phase), and then once, in parallel. A physical milestone, a phenomenon that several rules produce together, is one run of the engine with a fingerprint and a dated record, not a suite. The cost of checking is proportional to the risk of the change, never constant. (2026-09-19: the tests of the engine of events are listed below, "The engine of the law of events: what the tests show".)

**Open decision: what a returned field quantum does after spreading (2026-09-17).** A returning ray retraces its line by its step count (section 5.4); once the field spreads (feature 12) a field quantum's path is no longer one line, so a quantum that a Detector returns with 0 cannot walk back to its source that way, and a field ray has no event at which to perform an inverse split. The orchestrator's proposal, to be decided by the model owner before experiment A1: the returned quantum reverses on the line it arrived by and walks back until it is absorbed by the first content its coupling responds to or reaches its source, with no inverse split; every audit stays exact since nothing is created. Until decided, feature 12 implements exactly that and says so. Settled on 2026-09-18 by the law of the bit (section 5.4, points 3 and 6): a returned shadow turns back with its heading reversed and its momentum inverted and mixes at every Node like any shadow; there is no walk and no inverse split of a shadow, and it is absorbed wherever it reaches its owner. Settled again on 2026-09-18 (the evening) by the law of the shadow (section 5.4): there is no return; a quantum that reaches held content is absorbed, and a mark returns nothing. (2026-09-19, the law of events: closed, nothing returns; "So we do not want a return to the source.")

**The constants: what is derived and what is an input (2026-09-17).** Mass ratios are derivable: the ladder of hypothesis 12 is the set of loop-closing contents under the catalog's binding table, and experiment A10 counts them against the known spectrum, the proton-to-electron ratio first. The strength of the electric coupling and the strength of gravity are inputs today: the release ratio n/d of a field family and the modulus N of the lag register (section 3.28) are declared widths, and hypothesis 14's G = ħc/(N m₀)² is a reparametrization until something fixes N. The Born table's ratios were an input of the same kind until 2026-09-18, when the model owner ruled that the table is computed from the phase width N (section 5.4, point 17). Two open hypotheses record the question, 16 (what fixes the lag modulus: the top of the mass ladder, the resolution the spreading field needs, or nothing) and 17 (what fixes the release ratio and the table: the symmetry of section 3.27 and the path counting of 3.5, or nothing); until they are answered the model predicts forms, 1/N² and 1/r², and not the values of G and α. (2026-09-18: the field family is retired, point 12, the ratio being the size of a family's shadow set, "A thing does not emit"; the lag register is retired with point 22; the Born table is computed from N, point 17; what fixes G remains hypothesis 16's question.) (2026-09-18, the evening, the law of the shadow: the size of a shadow set became a rate with "A thing emits", ρ, the release per unit of held content per interval, the orchestrator's name, and it is the input for the strength of a force, the model owner's to choose; what fixes G remains hypothesis 16's question.) (Under the law of events, 2026-09-19: the inputs are the declared widths of the world file of 5.4, ρ, K, N, the suspension's width, the exchange rate of a paid family and the detectors' sensitivities; nothing else is free.) (Superseded on 2026-09-20 by the model owner on issue #369, section 5.4 and [record 106](LOG_2026-09-20.md#106-decided-the-masses-and-charges-are-the-initialisation-the-catalog-of-the-families-not-derivable-in-this-law-the-two-sentences-superseded-the-binding-design-pending): mass ratios are not derivable in this law; the masses and the charges of the families are the initialisation, the catalog's declared contents and charge per unit; the ladder of hypothesis 12 and A10's count are closed by decision; the strengths of the couplings stay inputs as stated.)

**Open decisions of the law of the shadow (2026-09-18, the evening; section 5.4).** What round 8 of DERIVATIONS.md must show before the new engine (feature 20, field-only-v1) is trusted, none of it assumed: that a held content is stable, its gravity and electricity free and its light paid; that its step, a release toward the neighbour and an absorption there, keeps it at or below the field's speed; that a content in uniform motion absorbs no net momentum from its own field, which is inertia; that bound contents exchanging quanta give the ladder of hypothesis 12; and what a split and a pair creation are as tables. Open with them, the orchestrator's list, flagged: what a mark that misses does, what the steering of two things of one family becomes, what a decay is as a table on held content, and what the siblings of one event are when nothing walks a path. Not open: Bell, two events of one field at two places, each born of the local field and the local setting, at most 2 (reading (vii)). The value of ρ, the rate of release, is the model owner's, as the size of the shadow set was. (2026-09-19, the evening, the model owner: "No confrontations are needed. Only tests that everything is as designed." The engine is the one engine without any of the above shown by a run; what round 8 was to show stays a list of research questions for runs made when wanted and registered in EXPERIMENTS.md, not a condition of anything; what round 8 did show on paper is in DERIVATIONS.md sections 51 to 56. The two choices decided as declared widths the same day, the phase turn in flight and the quantum of a family, are in 5.4's status line.) (2026-09-19, the law of events: recorded in 5.4 after "One speed, and what is seen" (the records moved to [LOG_2026-09-19.md](LOG_2026-09-19.md) on 2026-09-20, record 87) and implemented the same evening as `events-v1`, the one engine, the engine of the law of the shadow deleted; what it changes is listed there and in Highlights coverage, and what shows it is tests of the code, not runs.)

**The engine of the law of events: what the tests show (the orchestrator's list, 2026-09-19, flagged; implemented the same evening as `events-v1`, the modules named in [Highlights coverage](HIGHLIGHTS_IMPLEMENTATION.md)).** Tests of the code on minimal boards, no runs: (a) everything that enters a Node in an interval leaves it or is measured in the same interval, nothing kept at the Node; (b) a single quantum leaves whole to one side, its momentum's heading, wherever it is and whatever else is there of another number; (c) nine coherent units of one number entering on one Port leave four back and one on each other heading, and a large bundle within five units of the shares; (d) a suspended event counts down on itself, and the event behind it on the lane leaves after it, never before; (e) the books, in transit, measured and escaped, and the momentum over all records, balanced at every interval; (f) a measured event of content below the release's denominator still releases, at its rate off its clock, and a family's phase in transit advances its declared steps per Link; (g) the world file's refusals by name; (h) the pinned integers of the slit worlds re-pinned from the new engine, as a check that it does what the law says and not as a result. (Superseded on 2026-09-19 by the Beam Law, section 5.4: `events-v1` and its mixing of (b) and (c) are deleted, [MIGRATION](MIGRATION.md#the-law-of-the-ray-on-2026-09-19-rays-v1); what the engine as built does is [BEAM_LAW section 3](BEAM_LAW.md#3-the-nodes-interval-nature_beam) with its notes, and the tests are the ones the notes name.)

## 5.6 The glossary of the names and how each is computed (2026-09-20)

The model owner, 2026-09-20: "the architect is to make sure the names of the system are uniform, that none is redundant, and that each has a definition in Highlights of how it is computed." The architect's table follows, read from the code with every formula in integers as the code forms it; the last part lists the redundancies with the one name recommended, which the orchestrator adopts as the naming plan (executed with the NatureBeam rename). A name's row is the definition; the law's owner document stays RAY_LAW (renamed BEAM_LAW on 2026-09-20 with the names NatureBeam and GameBoard, record 44).

**As built at main 7e523c55 (PR #457, 2026-09-21).** The tables below are the architect's snapshot of 2026-09-20 at `1884b8b5` and `f88443f2`, kept as the dated definition of the names; what the engine does since is [BEAM_LAW section 3](BEAM_LAW.md#3-the-nodes-interval-nature_beam) and its notes ([note 10 onward](BEAM_LAW.md#10-implementation-notes-2026-09-19-the-implementation)), and it differs from the snapshot in these ways: the law is `"law": "beam"`, the record `beam-v1` (record 37); the interval's order is the frame, the measured events' steps, the law, then the clocks' turn and count, and a row and a body meet once at the crossing of their world lines, the key `doppler` and the grain G deleted (the crossing rule, note 48); every count of a body is `by_drive` on an accumulator of its own record, `by_clock` its constant-rate identity, the counts one table on the record, the flight rule the position's accumulator off the age with the name "flight table" retired, no remainder discarded at run time (note 17 as amended, note 41); the push is the signed inner product over the columns with the reader's charges read at the frame, the row carrying nothing of the emitter but its number (`charge` and `mass` gone from the row), the lifetime, the held content and the contact through the table (notes 27, 28, 31); `wave` is the default reading (note 29); the age is whole on the record, the age moment and `age_bound` built (note 25); the window's width, a paid family's charge per unit of amount, `become` and the W (note 36); the meeting under the key `meeting` (note 35); every lamp births records and the one click reads them, the key `amplitude` and the crowd's pointer threshold deleted (note 37 (x)); the hand (note 39); binding-v1's give (note 40); the group structure named (note 42); the mass of a bound set and the parity (notes 43, 44); the crowd audit (note 47); the exact phase at the click, the phase at the exact time of the row's last Link, read at every end of a row (note 45); the birth wheel, `wheel` [r, W] declared on every lamp with no default, u = ordinal x r mod W the record's coordinate on the ladder with the rungs on W (note 46); the click's weight the bilinear form on the record's phase-count vector with the Gram matrix of the tables, the pointer a report (note 37 (xii)). Not on main at that commit: form B (record 301), covariant-readings-v1, optical-v1, colour-v1 and the event-driven scheduler (decided, not built).

Architecture review, 2026-09-20, for the model owner's glossary. Read from the
code at `1884b8b5` (branch `claude/universe24-new-3ytqde`; the detector as a
set with one record merged at 00:06 UTC) with the formulas checked line by
line at `f88443f2` and re-read where the merge changed them. Every formula is
in integers as the code forms it; `//` is the floor division, `isqrt` the
integer root, `mod` the remainder. "Same name" says whether the code, the
tests, the tools, the examples and the live documents (RAY_LAW, ENGINE,
TERMINOLOGY, the READMEs) use one name; the aliases found are listed and the
recommended single name is in the last section. Bounds: every amount, content,
momentum component, K and tick is refused above 2^62 - 1 (`AMOUNT_BOUND`,
`MOMENTUM_BOUND`); a charge and a quantum above 2^30 - 1 (`MAX_VALUE`).

### World-file keys (`events/world.py`, `parse_ray_world`)

| `model_id` | `parse_ray_world` | a nonempty string, the world's identity | ENGINE "The world" | no: `run.json` writes it as `model` |

| `boundary` | `_boundary` | `"open"` or `{x,y,z: "open"|"periodic"}`, missing axes open; `periodic[axis]` true wraps the walk (`coordinates[:, axis] mod extent`); an open face is the face detector `face:+x` .. `face:-z` | ENGINE "Per-axis board topology" | yes; `periodic` (parsed tuple), `boundary_per_axis` (preflight) |
| `ticks` | `parse_ray_world`; `execute_ray_run` | the intervals to run, 0 .. 2^62 - 1; `tick` is the interval's index from 1 | ENGINE "The record" | no: "tick" (code, records, tools) and "interval" (documents, Highlights) name the same step |
| `K` | `parse_nature_beam_world` -> `NatureBeamWorld.K` (as declared) and `turn_rate` = (n, d) | the turn as the release at the rate [n, d] phase steps per unit of content per self-creation: `s = NatureBeamWorld.turn(clock_age, content) = by_clock(clock_age, content x n, d)`; an integer K in the file is the pair [1, K], bit-identical (the four unifications, 2026-09-20) | BEAM_LAW 2, 3 step 5, note 33; ENGINE "A release costs" | one name: `K` in the file and the record, `turn_rate` the pair it resolves to |

### The ray's record (`NatureBeam`; the store's columns, all int64)

| Name | Computed in | Definition | Defined in | Same name; aliases |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `node` | walk; `RayStore.flat` | the flat index `x (Y Z) + y Z + z`; after a step `coordinates + step_d(age)`, wrapped on a periodic axis; through an open face the row leaves (a face click) | RAY_LAW 2, 3 step 1 | no: `position` (x, y, z) in `NatureBeam`, `state.json` and the measured event |
| `direction` | collision only | an index of D; the label's and the flight's direction; changed only by the collision table (a single unit in a slot) | RAY_LAW 2, 4 | no: "heading" for the six unit vectors (indices 2 .. 7, `PORT_HEADINGS`); `arrival` (the direction the row arrived on this interval, `HERE` = 0 if it did not step) |
| `age` | walk | the flight phase: `(age + 1) mod L_d` at every interval for a moving direction; kept for a rest direction; 0 at birth; in flight: kept whole, the flight reading `age mod L_d` | RAY_LAW 2, 3, note 2 | no: `Measured.age` is the clock (self-creations); `clock_age` its value before the frame's advance |
| `phase` | walk | `(phase + phase_per_link) mod N` at every Link crossed; stamped by the emitter at birth; kept by a re-emission | RAY_LAW 2 | yes |
| `number` | birth | the last emitter's number; a re-emission stamps the re-emitter's | RAY_LAW 2 | yes; "owner" (`RayWorld.owners`), "emitter" (prose) |
| `amount` | merge | whole units; identical rows are one row with the amounts added (`merge`, bounded) | RAY_LAW 2, note 3 | no: "units" (ledger `face_units`, `escaped_units`, prose "in units") |
| `content` | birth | the content one unit carries: `h x s` for a paid ray, 0 for a free one; an identity field of the merge | RAY_LAW 2 | no: "carried" (`t_carried`, `content_absorbed`: amount x content), `held`, `mass` |
| `charge` | birth (step 5) | a free family's ray: the emitter's charge at birth; 0 on a paid ray | RAY_LAW 2, note 20 | yes |
| `record`, `branch`, multiplicity `m` | birth (step 5), the split, the normal form | under the key: the record's number (the lamp's number x 2^32 + its ordinal), the row's label within it, and the multiplicity m with the amplitude amount / sqrt(m) x v(phase) and the norm amount^2 / m exact; 0, 0, 1 without the key and not written | BEAM_LAW note 37 (i) | yes |
| `mass` | birth (step 5) | a free family's ray: the emitter's `held[family]` at birth (M_B, the electric divisor); 0 on a paid ray; 0 also on a re-emitted ray of a family the re-emitter holds none of (finding B2) | RAY_LAW 2, note 20 | no: the quantity is a content (`held`, `amount`, "M_B"); the column is called `mass` |

| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |

| `per_port` | `Readings.per_port` | the amount that crossed INTO each Node through each Port this interval (`heading_port(step)`), a walk diagnostic for Gauss's flux, not a moment | ENGINE "Diagnostic scope" | yes; "Links crossed" |
| the amplitude | `coherent_pointer` | `32 x amount` per row (`AMPLITUDE_SCALE`) | RAY_LAW 5, note 3 | no: "amplitude", "the 32nds" |
| the pointer (X, Y) | `coherent_pointer` | over the rows a set clicked in the interval: `X = sum 32 amount C[phase]`, `Y = sum 32 amount S[phase]` with C, S the 1/256 tables of `core/phase.py`; the first moment of the one reading over the circle (`read_groups` on the vectors (C[phase], S[phase], 0) with the weights 32 x amount, the four unifications, 2026-09-20); in the register while `reading_fits`, the same table in Python integers beyond (the record is never refused) | BEAM_LAW 5, notes 19 and 33 | no: "pointer", "coherent pointer", "coherent sum", "coherent reading", "the wave", "amplitude" (prose) |

| the age moment (in flight) | not in the code | `sum amount x age` over the arrivals of the reading set, `reads: "age"` | Highlights 5.4 | not yet |
