| `escaped` | `ledger.held_escaped`, `face_*` | measured events that stepped off (`held`), and for the transit and content lines the faces' sums `escaped_units`, `escaped_content` | ENGINE | no: "units" vs "amount"; "face" tallies |
| transit `released` | `ledger.transit_released` | `+= sum amount` of the rows born (releases, lamps, re-emissions, home) | ENGINE | yes |
| transit `absorbed` | `ledger.transit_absorbed` | `+= amount` of the rows a table took (home, click, re-release); `-= pending` of an event that stepped off | ENGINE | yes |
| content line | `content_released`, `content_absorbed` | the same identities in `amount x content` | ENGINE | no: "carried" |
| `home` | `Measured.measured[f]["home"]`, `pending` | the own number's arrivals, taken to be created again: the tally, and `home`/`home_content` = `pending_amount`/`pending_content` on the state | RAY_LAW 3 step 4, note 4 | yes; `pending` (code) |
| momentum `measured` | `books` | `sum entry.momentum` over the measured events | ENGINE | yes |
| momentum `transit` | `ledger.transit_momentum`; `recount` | the running sum of the labels born less the labels that left (escaped, home, absorbed); `recount`: `sum momentum_labels` over every row | note 22 | yes |
| momentum `escaped` | `ledger.face_momentum` | the labels of the rows that left through each face plus the momentum of the events that stepped off | ENGINE | yes |
| momentum `turned` | `ledger.turned_momentum` | per family and the world total: the change of the transit momentum line by weight x (u_d' - u_d) at every turn of the meeting (`meeting-v1`, 2026-09-20), a report as `pushed` is; zero without the key | BEAM_LAW note 35; ENGINE "The books" | yes |
| `balanced` | `books` | per family `initial + measured == current + spent + escaped` (measured), `initial + released == current + escaped + absorbed` (transit, content); `conserved_at_every_completed_tick` = all | ENGINE | yes; a ledger identity, not a store check (`recount=True` is) |
| `charge` | `books` | `sum entry.charge` over the measured events, plus (D-1, 2026-09-20) the paid rows in transit and the paid units escaped (`ledger.units_escaped`), conserved through a click, a home and an escape | BEAM_LAW note 36 (ii) | yes |

### Tables and functions that hold a rule

| Name | Where | Definition | Defined in | Same name; aliases |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Q | `nature_beam.Q` | 64, the flight table's resolution (and, in flight, the label's scale) | RAY_LAW 3 | yes |
| S_1 | `flight_table` -> `manhattan` | `|a| + |b| + |c|` of a direction | RAY_LAW 3 | no: `manhattan` (code) |
| T_d | `flight_table` -> `turns` | `isqrt(3 (a^2 + b^2 + c^2) Q^2)`; T_d >= S_1 Q | RAY_LAW 3 | no: the array is named `turns`, a homonym of the clock's `turn` |
| m(tau) | `FlightTable.manhattan_steps` | `(2 tau S_1 Q + T_d) // (2 T_d)`; `m(tau + 1) - m(tau)` in {0, 1} | RAY_LAW 3 | yes |
| line_d | `_bresenham` -> `lines` | S_1 unit steps: at step j the axis maximizing `|v_i| (j + 1) - S_1 |position_i|`, the lowest axis first | RAY_LAW 3 | yes; "the digital line" |
| step_d(tau) | `flight_table` -> `steps` (int8) | `line_d[m(tau) mod S_1]` if `m(tau + 1) > m(tau)`, else (0, 0, 0); the inverse subtracts `step_d(tau - 1)` | RAY_LAW 3 | yes; "the walk" |
| L_d | `flight_table` -> `period` | `p = T_d // gcd(S_1 Q, T_d)`; `per = S_1 Q p // T_d`; `L_d = p x (S_1 // gcd(per, S_1))`; 1 for a rest direction | RAY_LAW 3 | no: `period` (code) |
| the collision table | `collision_table` | states `s in {0, 1, 2}^8` (empty, single = one row of amount 1, crowd), code `sum s_k 3^k`; class `(crowd mask, n singles, S = sum of the singles' headings)`; members sorted as 8-tuples; forward = the cyclic shift +1, inverse -1; applied per (Node, number, content) class to rows with `direction < 8` at a Node without a measured event; a moved unit takes its new slot's direction by rank | RAY_LAW 4, note 18 | yes; "slot" = heading index 0 .. 5 or rest 6, 7 (`slot = 6 + direction` for a rest row, `direction - 2` for a heading) |
| `label_weights` | `nature_beam` | `amount` (free) or `content x amount` (paid), refused if `max|amount| x max|content| > 2^62 - 1` | RAY_LAW 2 | yes |
| `momentum_labels` | `nature_beam` | `label_weights x D[direction]` per row (in int64; the product is not bounded before it is formed: finding B1) | RAY_LAW 2, note 18 | no: "label", "momentum", the record key `push` on a click (a label), `momentum` on a face click and a step |
| the push (`push_form`) | inline in `nature_beam` step 4 (no function of that name exists) | per (reader A, group of number B): `V = sum label` over the group's rows; free ray: `push = -V x content_A` plus, if `charge_A != 0`, per (q_B, M_B) class `t = V_class x q_A x q_B` (bounded), `push += sign(t) x by_clock(age_A, |t|, M_B)`; paid ray: `push = V`; `momentum_A += push`, `pushed_A += push` (each bounded) | RAY_LAW 3 step 4, note 20 | no: `push_form` is named in RAY_LAW, ENGINE, TERMINOLOGY, MIGRATION and HIGHLIGHTS_IMPLEMENTATION but no such function exists; "push", "push taken", "pushed", "recoil" |
| `by_clock(age, n, d)` | `core/integer.py` | `((age + 1) n) // d - (age n) // d`, d >= 1; exact on average, no remainder kept | RAY_LAW 3; ENGINE | yes; "off the clock", "rate", "the whole part"; a re-export in `engine.by_clock` |
| `apportion_whole(total, weights, first)` | `core/integer.py` | `share_k = total w_k // sum w`; the `total - sum share` units left go to the largest remainders, ties from index `first` in cyclic index order | RAY_LAW 3 step 5 | yes |
| the turn s | `engine._frame_all` | `by_clock(clock_age, content, K)`; refused if `2 s >= N`; the phase then `(phase + s) mod N` | ENGINE "The frame" | no: `turn` (code) vs `FlightTable.turns` (T_d); `Measured.phase_steps` (the cumulative turn) vs `RayWorld.phase_steps` (N) |
| the clock's count | `engine._suspend` | `owed = by_clock(clock_age, presence x n, d)`; paid one per interval; no self-creation, release, turn or step while owed > 0; `waited` counts the intervals | ENGINE | no: "owed", "count", "suspension" |
| the presence | `_family_plan` | `sum amount` of every row at the reader's Node of another number, every family, rest and moving alike; `Measured.presence` | RAY_LAW 3 step 2 | no: "scalar" |
| the step | `engine._move` | above (`width`); `adjacent_node` gives the target; a target with a measured event refuses (the step counted); an open face: the event leaves (a face click with its `held` and `pending`) | ENGINE | yes; "refused step" |
| the threshold | `_family_plan` | over the set's arrivals of other numbers, `sum amount < threshold` -> every row passes with a `pass` record naming `threshold` | RAY_LAW 5 | yes |
| the window | `_family_plan` | per row (or per set under `wave`): `tables.window[(phase - s) mod N]`; outside -> a `pass` record naming `window` | RAY_LAW 3 step 4, note 5 | yes |
| the detector's record | `_family_plan`, `_apply_plan` | `wave`: `record += X^2 + Y^2` per set per family per interval (Python integers); `beam`: `record += the count clicked` after the pairing | RAY_LAW 5, note 19; ENGINE (1884b8b5) | no: "record", "squared record", "coherent record"; the `record` line's `record` field |
| C[k], S[k] | `core/phase.py` | fixed-point cosine and sine of `2 pi k / N` in 256ths from an integer series (`_PI_FIXED` 3141592654 / 10^9), cached per N; `C^2 + S^2 <= 65897 < 257^2` | RAY_LAW 5 | yes; "the 1/256 tables" |
| `exact_sum`, `exact_column_sums`, `grouped_sums` | `nature_beam` | an int64 reduction taken in the register when `max|v| x count <= 2^62 - 1`, in Python integers otherwise | note 19, 22 | yes |
| `bounded(value, entry, quantity)` | `nature_beam` | refuses `|value| > 2^62 - 1` naming the measured event, its Node and the quantity | ENGINE "The books" | yes |
| `merge_key` | `RayStore` | the identity fields (node, direction, age, phase, number, content, charge, mass) packed as `key = (key << width_k) + (column - min)` when `sum width_k <= 62`, else the lexsort of the same fields | note 22 | yes; "the packed key" |

### The run's record

| Name | Written by | Keys | Defined in | Same name; aliases |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `run.json` | `execute_ray_run` | `package_version`, `source_sha256`, `initialization_sha256`, `law`, `model`, `shape`, `boundary`, `K`, `N`, `release`, `suspension`, `width`, `directions` (the table beyond the rest and the headings), `families[{name, quantum, charge, phase, phase_per_link}]`, `numbers{n: {position, family}}`, `status`, `error`, `requested_ticks`, `completed_ticks`, `tick`, `elapsed_seconds`, `conserved_at_every_completed_tick`, `audit[books per tick]`, `measured_content[]`, `transit_content[]`, `momentum[]`, `measured[Measured.state()]`, `detectors[]` (name, nodes, threshold, reading, families{measured, clicks, record, phase}; then the faces: measured, clicks, content, record, measured_content; momentum), `escaped[{family, amount, content, momentum}]`, `display`, `initialization_resolution` | ENGINE "The record" | `model` vs `model_id`; `escaped[].momentum` is the total over families, repeated per family |
| `Measured.state()` | `measured.py` | `number, position, family (index), held, content, phase, charge, momentum, fixed, windows, detector (index), age, owed, home, home_content, waited, phase_steps, steps, measured{home, read, measure, rerelease}, events, pushed, contacts, became, widths` (`record` moved to the set at 1884b8b5; `contacts` the strong force, `became` and `widths` the weak force) | ENGINE | `events` holds the clicks; `family`/`detector` are indices where the records elsewhere carry names |
| `state.json` | `snapshot_writer` from `snapshot_stream` | `law, tick, shape, boundary, measured[], detectors[], escaped[], nodes[{position, families[{family (name), rays[{direction (vector), age, phase, number, amount, content, (charge, mass)}]}]}]` | ENGINE "The record" | `position` vs `node`; `direction` a vector here, an index in the store |
| `events.jsonl` kinds | `nature_beam`, `engine._move` | `home` (tick, node, measured, detector, family, number, amount, push = the labels in, content); `pass` (…, amount, phase, window, threshold); `read` and `rerelease` (…, number = the other, amount, push, content, reading); `click` at a detector (…, amount, push = the row's label, phase, content, reading); `click` on a face (node, measured None or the event's number, detector `face:*`, family, number, amount, phase, momentum, content; for an event also held, home, home_content); `record` (tick, node/measured or None for a set of several, detector, family, number 0, record, pointer under `wave`, phase); `step` (tick, number, node, to, momentum); `become` (tick, node, measured, trigger, triggered, from, into, products, recoil, counted; `weak-v1`); under the key `birth`, `split`, `cancel`, `gather` (the record, the set, the node, the rungs, the weight, the total, the windows), `rotate` and `gate` lines (`amplitude-v1`) | ENGINE "The record" | `push` on a click is a label, `momentum` on a face click is the same label; `measured` on a line is the reader's number, `number` the emitter's |

### Redundancies and inconsistencies, with the one name recommended

1. **presence / scalar / count** — one quantity, three names (`Reading.scalar`, `Measured.presence`, `Readings.presence`; `Readings.count` is `outside`; "count" also names `owed` and the `beam` record). Recommend: `presence` for the scalar everywhere (`reads: "presence"`), `arrived` for `outside`, `owed` for the clock, `clicks` for the beam record.
2. **flow / vector** — `Reading.vector`, `Readings.flow`, "net flow", "label moment". Recommend: `flow` (`reads: "flow"`), and `label_flow` for V.
3. **pointer / amplitude / coherent sum / coherent reading / the wave** — one (X, Y). Recommend: `pointer` (with `amplitude` for the row's `32 x amount` only).
4. **here / rest** — `here` is the reading of rows that did not step (rest rays and dwelling rays); a rest direction is index 0 or 1; `HERE = 0` reuses the rest index as the arrival code. Recommend: `here` for the reading, `rest` for the two directions, a distinct constant `NO_ARRIVAL` for the code.
5. **suspension / owed / the count / the clock's count** — recommend: `suspension` for the key, `owed` for the state and the prose.
6. **label / momentum / push** — the row's label is written as `push` on a detector click and as `momentum` on a face click and a step; `push` should mean a push taken. Recommend: `momentum` on every record line that carries a label; `push` only in `read`/`rerelease`/`home` (what the reader took).
7. **kind / quantum / KINDS** — `kind` is deleted for families; `configuration_validation.KINDS` is the preflight's kind of document. Recommend: rename `KINDS` to `DOCUMENT_KINDS` or drop it (one kind).
8. **heading / direction** — keep `direction` for a ray's field and the table; `heading` only for the six unit vectors (`PORT_HEADINGS`, the collision's slots).
9. **tick / interval** — the code and the records say `tick`; the documents and Highlights say "interval". Recommend: `tick` in every record key and identifier, "interval" as the prose noun defined once in TERMINOLOGY as "the step tick t -> t + 1".
10. **turn / turns / phase_steps** — `FlightTable.turns` (T_d) is a homonym of the clock's `turn`; `Measured.phase_steps` (the cumulative turn) is a homonym of `RayWorld.phase_steps` (N). Recommend: `FlightTable.resolution` for T_d, `Measured.turned` for the cumulative turn, `N` kept as `phase_steps`.
11. **K / clock** — resolved on 2026-09-20: the key `K` is `NatureBeamWorld.K` as declared and resolves to the pair `turn_rate`; the turn is the release at that rate.
12. **width** — the push's `width`, the phase window's "width" (TERMINOLOGY), the detector set's "width" (Highlights), the suspension's "fractional width". Recommend: `width` for the push only; "window" for `phase_window`; "extent" for a set's Nodes; "rate" for `suspension`.
13. **amount / content / held / mass / carried / measured_content** — `amount` is units for a ray and content for a measured event; `mass` on a ray is the emitter's `held`; `carried` is `amount x content`. Recommend: `content` for a measured event's key (in place of `amount`), `emitter_content` for the column `mass`, `carried` documented once as `amount x content`.
14. **units / amount** — `face_units`, `escaped_units`, "in units". Recommend: `amount`.
15. **events / clicks** — `Measured.events` holds the clicks (`run.json` says `clicks`). Recommend: `clicks`.
16. **node / position** — `node` on event lines and in the store, `position` in `state.json`, the world and `Measured`. Recommend: `node` for the triple in every record (TERMINOLOGY: the Node is the location).
17. **model / model_id** — `run.json` writes `model`. Recommend: `model_id`.
18. **measured** — the run's list, the ledger line, the tally by rule and the reader's number on an event line. Recommend: `reader` for the event line's field (its partner `number` -> `emitter`), `taken` for the tally, `measured` for the line and the list.
19. **RULES vs TABLES** — `measured.RULES` (`home, read, measure, rerelease`, the tally keys) is not the rule set (`world.TABLES`: `read, measure, rerelease, pass`). Recommend: `TALLIES`.
20. **reads / reading / Reading / Readings / read** — five near-homonyms. Recommend: `reads` (the component key), `reading` (the detector's law, `beam`/`wave`), `Moments` for the class `Reading`, `BoardDiagnostics` for `Readings`, and the record field `reading` -> `component`.
21. **push_form** — five documents name a function that does not exist; the push is computed inline in `nature_beam` step 4. Recommend: either extract `push_form(...)` as the documents say (one place, the same integers) or correct the five documents to "the push of step 4 in `nature_beam`".
22. **Leftovers of the deleted engines in live names**: `test_repository_hygiene.duplicate_files(ignore_run_duration)` keys on `disturbance_types`; `OLD_KEYS` (23 refused keys) and `EVENTS_KEYS`, `REVERSIBLE_KEYS` are refusal tables (keep, they name the law in the refusal); `ui.py`'s renderer CSP and `.html` branch (audit A9); the `events` package name (kept by the design: a ray is an event in transit); `Sec-Fetch-Site` is an HTTP header, not the deleted noun.
23. **`face:*` as a user detector name** — accepted by the parser, two `face:+x` in the record. Recommend: refuse a detector name in `FACE_NAMES`.

## 5.7 From the world we see to the vector world: the conversions, the principles, what is derived and what is input (2026-09-21)

The model owner, 2026-09-21 (translated): "update the Highlights immediately on the system: mainly how the conversions work and why, from the physical world we see to the world of vectors; the principles; what is derived, what is needed and why." Written by the Boss from the decisions of 2026-09-21 (records 173 to 198 of [LOG_2026-09-20.md](LOG_2026-09-20.md)); the mathematics is in [DERIVATIONS_BEAM.md](DERIVATIONS_BEAM.md) and [vector_form/LAW.md](designs/vector_form/LAW.md); the law's code contract stays [BEAM_LAW.md](BEAM_LAW.md). Notation: a scalar plain, a vector in bold lowercase, a matrix or an operator in bold uppercase, every symbol named at its first use.

**The statement** (the owner, record 181). The state is integer vectors on tori and one tensor; the law is one map **F** (the interval's map) at every Node, made of six operations; the GameBoard computes exactly where the formula has only a limit; the measurement is the one threshold read out.

**The three conversions**, how the world we see becomes vectors and comes back:

1. *From a thing to a vector.* A row (a message in flight) is a point of a product of circles and lines: its Node (the GameBoard's translation group Z_X x Z_Y x Z_Z), its direction (an index of the fan F_P), its phase (the circle Z_N), its age, its amount and its number. A record (an apparatus's one message) is the vector **f** of Z^N of the amounts that ended at each phase per (end Node, label), an element of the group ring Z[Z_N]. A body (a measured event) is its held contents, its momentum vector **p**, and its counts table, one accumulator (s, r, d) per count: the drive, the turn, the owed count, the push per column, the release, the lamp's wheel. A Node is an index and holds nothing (record 155).
2. *From the world to a reading.* Nobody sees the state. A detector reads its Node's neighbourhood by one linear reading **R**: the moments of the arriving rows, order 0 a scalar (the presence), order 1 a vector (the flow, the pointer), order 2 a tensor (the spread) and the age moment; the same content as physics' stress-energy tensor **T** (density, current, stress): a detector reads **T**, never a metric (record 196).
3. *From a reading to a bit.* The click is one bilinear form on the record, the weight **f**^T **G f** with **G** = **E**^T **E** the Gram matrix of the rounded cosine and sine tables, then one threshold [u < b_k] against the rungs of the cells' cumulative weights, u the lamp's wheel written on the record at its birth (a scalar on the circle Z_W; the wheel decided, [record 180](LOG_2026-09-20.md#180-the-wheel-as-the-generic-vector-form-the-owner-2026-09-21-about-0105z-the-owners-words-translated-the-wheel-was-also-turned-into-a-generic-vector-wasnt-it-the-bosss-yes-taken-as-the-decision-on-record-163-3-the-birth-wheel-is-one-row-of-the-counts-table-on-the-lamps-record-acc--2531-acc-mod-4096-the-translation-on-the-torus-z--4096-that-every-count-is-the-lamp-writes-the-rows-value-on-the-record-at-birth-as-u-at-the-click-the-cells-cumulative-weights-are-sums-the-rungs-2-w-c_k--t--2-t-one-declared-rounding-and-the-choice-u--b_k-the-wall-test-so-the-whole-click-is-a-multiplication-by-declared-matrices-an-addition-an-inner-product-and-a-threshold-no-step-of-its-own-the-map-record-164-showed-the-same-clicks-as-the-bit-reversed-ordinal-within-one-click-at-4096-births-physically-the-lamps-phase-at-the-moment-of-birth-at-a-rate-incommensurate-with-its-birth-rate-built-as-item-3-of-the-click-pull-request-after-the-exact-phase), on main at 7e523c55 (PR #457, 2026-09-21): `wheel` [r, W] declared on every lamp, no default, u = ordinal x r mod W, BEAM_LAW note 46). The amplitudes are one factorisation of **G**, not a step of the law (records 173, 188, 192). The click discards information by design, the law's one cost (the information law, DERIVATIONS_BEAM.md section 9).

**The six operations**, the only verbs, of which every registered integer is made: the translation of an accumulator by its rate (s <- s + r: the flight, the phase, the age, every count); the bilinear form with a declared matrix (the coupling **C a**, the click's **G**, the moments); the group-ring addition (the merge in Z[Z_N], an antiphase pair cancelling); the permutation (the collision, the gate); the evaluation (the tables at zeta_N, the primitive N-th root of unity); the Euclidean division with the remainder kept (the carry that is the event, and the threshold). Two places are not linear and there is no third: the carry (a bijection, nothing lost) and the click's threshold (the one read-out). A root (the meeting's norm at run time, the contraction gamma needs) is outside the six: the seventh verb, the owner's open question (record 197); decided since ([record 249](LOG_2026-09-20.md#249-decided-the-owner-2026-09-21-about-0430z-the-owners-words-translated-so-i-do-not-need-to-choose-a-or-b-i-can-choose-both-a-and-b-describe-them-to-me-the-lorentz-decision-is-a-and-b-a-the-law-stays-the-six-verbs-and-its-prediction-that-a-bodys-own-counter-does-not-slow-with-speed-is-stated-as-a-falsifiable-prediction-a-fail-against-the-muons-lifetime-in-flight-in-naturemd-a-row-the-theory-owns-b-lorentz-v1-the-seventh-verb-a-root-at-a-declared-grain-slowing-a-bodys-own-counter-by-gamma-and-contracting-its-bond-is-built-beside-the-law-under-its-own-identity-as-a-comparison-hypothesis-formmd-section-4-states-it-so-that-it-can-fail-the-trigger-ticks-71-and-126-of-j4s-muon-at-043-c-and-086-c-against-the-laws-64-both-run-against-the-same-pins-the-muon-of-j4-the-thrown-orbit-of-12b-series-es-probe-of-12c-the-paper-stating-both-and-the-owner-choosing-which-leads-the-highlights-54-line-written-the-build-of-lorentz-v1-queued-for-the-implementer-after-form-b-the-crossing-rule-first-with-the-physics-rule-review-and-the-registers-derivations-entries-the-owners-second-direction-in-the-same-message-translated-try-to-add-the-quarks-too-model-them-and-bring-them-in-so-that-we-try-to-give-their-masses-and-their-properties-as-well-bring-them-into-our-groups-everything-enters-the-groups-as-a-group-or-a-set-or-a-vector-within-our-generic-laws-bring-the-quarks-in-with-all-their-families-and-check-how-it-converges-assigned-a-physicists-read-only-design-first-docsdesignsquarksquarksmd-on-a-branch-in-git-the-six-quarks-as-families-of-the-family-table-under-the-generic-law-the-rational-charges-2-3-and--1-3-per-unit-of-content-the-masses-in-units-per-record-243-the-strong-column-sigma-the-lifetime-where-a-decay-is-known-what-the-law-lacks-for-confinement-colour-as-a-property-with-three-values-or-the-quarks-own-field-rays-binding-hypotheses-13-a-third-column-or-none-and-whether-it-passes-the-three-tests-the-nucleon-as-three-measured-events-bound-by-the-strong-column- **The principles**, why the conversions are right:

- *Locality.* A Node reads its six neighbours once per interval (LOCALITY-1), fixed work for a fixed K; the causal bound, k Links in at least k intervals, is an executable test (PR #415).
- *Straightness and one pace.* A row of direction D follows the digital line of D by the flight's Manhattan accumulator (rate 2 S_1 Q, wall 2 T_D, three deficits). c = 1 / sqrt 3 Links per interval is the norm of that operator: the largest isotropic pace at which no direction crosses two Links in one interval, Cauchy-Schwarz with equality on the cube diagonals; derived, not declared (records 186, 191).
- *Nothing at a Node, nothing discarded.* Every count is an accumulator on a record with its remainder kept; the flight table's cache, the doppler-v1 key and the constant G leave (records 155, 158; both landed on 2026-09-21, PRs #438 and #468).
- *Rows and bodies, not light and matter.* One motion primitive: a body's drive is the flight operator at the fraction its momentum earns (form B), so no body outruns a row and the motion is isotropic (records 183, 186); form B is decided and in build ([record (4)).
- *Equations first, runs as confirmation* (the main course, record 176). The linear block (rows) has a closed form and its clicks are computed before the run; the feedback block (a body whose rates read the crowd) is iterated by the GameBoard, per interval as built and over events once the scheduler to the next carry is built (record 190; decided and not built, record 191), and its continuum limit is the derivation's equation, which a run confirms with exact integers.
- *The generic vector form first* (record 177). A rule is written as the operation of a declared matrix, table or rate before its physical name; the engine branches on no name.
- *After the detector* (record 205). An experiment names, before the run, the vector or tensor it will read and its form: the record exposes scalars (counts, phases, amounts, ages), integer 3-vectors in declared units (**p** the momentum vector, **f** the net flow vector, a Node as a position), the traceless second-moment tensor **T**, and the record's phase-count vector; these readings are the only kind reality has.
- *A formula gives, a run proves* (record 205). The expectation of every experiment is derived from the law's operations before the run and registered with its derivation beside the number; the run compares, equal or a bounded deviation with its cause named; a run without a derived expectation is a research run and says so; the derivation mathematician's targets are the source of the series' expectations, a target not reached marking a quantity a run may only measure.

**What is derived**, from the six operations and the register's integers alone ([DERIVATIONS_BEAM.md](DERIVATIONS_BEAM.md)): c; Newton and Coulomb from the bilinear coupling; Doppler from the crossing rule; Born's rule as the unique reading (the lattice Gleason, 6.5: rotation-invariant, conserved at the balanced splitter and non-negative forces a positive quadratic form, the power 2 register-pinned to [1.917, 2.489)); Young's spacing and Bohr's levels; the delay field's equation; the information cost and the classical-quantum boundary (the click); special relativity as the symmetry of the limit. And what is not: gamma (the Lorentz factor) is not one of the six, a moving clock reads the bond's own slowing (1.383 x rest at 0.43 c against 1.107), a different law (record 178); the harmonic constants of the click's form (register-pinned); the masses (PREDICTIONS 26).

**What is input, and why** (record 189):

1. *The grain of the computation*, constants of the law and not of the world: N (the circle of phases), Q (the pace's grain), P (the sphere of directions), W (the birth wheel, `wheel` [r, W] on every lamp since 7e523c55), G (the fan's grain; no constant of the engine as built at main, the grain G = 2^12 of doppler-v1 being deleted with the crossing rule, BEAM_LAW note 48), K (the clock's pair) and the declared roundings at load (the tables at 1/256, T_D by isqrt, u_d). They are resolutions: in the limit the derivation reaches the known formulas; the law's claim is that the world's grain is finite, and PREDICTIONS 26 says what it quantises (what lives on a compact group: phases, directions), never the masses.
2. *The table of families and the width*, the physical inputs: per family the content (the mass number, on Z, free), the cost h (E = h f), the charge per unit of content rho, the strong column sigma, the lifetime L, the phase rate n / d, the hand; and the width S (the push per unit of content per unit of flow, which carries what physics calls Newton's G). These are the numbers physics measures and does not derive either.
3. *The state and the apparatus*: the GameBoard's extent and boundaries, the bodies' positions, momenta and held contents, the rows placed, the lamps' rates and directions, the detectors' thresholds and windows, the splitters' matrices, the gates.

If the law is right, the input to the real world is the family table, the initial state and one grain; c, h and G are units there as here.

**The three tests of every rule** (the owner, 2026-09-21, record 202), the admission test of the law, applied by the reviewer to every rule before it enters and by the implementer before it is written: (1) *generic*: one primitive with declared integers (a rate, a wall, a matrix, a table) and no family name or kind, the same primitive serving every family, its special cases values and not branches (a row is a body of no content), the engine branching on no name; (2) *vector*: one of the six verbs on the state vector, its rate at most bilinear in the state, no root, no float, no rounding at run time beyond the ones declared at load; (3) *local*: reading only its own record and the six neighbouring Nodes, fixed work and storage for a fixed K, nothing kept at a Node, every host reading labelled host. A rule that fails one test does not enter the law; a hypothesis that needs more (a seventh verb) is stated under its own identity, outside the law, until it passes or the owner admits the verb. Form B was the first rule read against the three (record 201).

**Why the three tests give the least computation in the large system, and a generic system ruled by small conditions** (the owner's question, 2026-09-21, record 206). True in this sense: the cost of an exact run is the number of events times a constant, and nothing in it grows with the GameBoard's size or the number of families; the number of events is the floor of any exact computation (each carry is a change of the state that must be made at least once), and below it lies only the linear block's closed form, which the vector test provides. *Local* bounds the work per Node per interval (six neighbours, a fixed number of accumulators for a fixed K), so the total is linear in the active Nodes and, between events, in the events; a rule reading beyond its neighbours would cost the board's size per Node. *Vector* makes every step a fixed number of bounded-integer operations with no iteration inside a step, no root, no solver, no transcendental function, gives the rows a closed form (one operation per event, not per interval), and lets the same operation run on every row at once. *Generic* makes one primitive serve every family, one code path and one table, so the cost per event is constant across kinds, the large world costing per event what the small one costs, and one primitive is verified instead of one per family. The limits: the count of events is what it is; the several-arm click is evaluated through its rank-2 factorisation, not the tensor form; the host's own costs (the state's writing, the record's format) are outside the law. And it follows that a generic system is ruled by small conditions: under the three tests the only free numbers are the family table's small declared integers, the width and the initial state; a large system has no parameter and no rule of its own scale (sections 3.21 and 3.22), so every large-scale law is the iterate of the small local rule and every known formula its limit; a change of one small condition propagates through the one primitive to every scale and moves the registered integers everywhere, exactly and never as noise, which is the system's falsifiability.

**The transformation from the GameBoard to the observed values** (the owner, 2026-09-21, record 210). Every value we see in the world is a detector's reading, so a quantity of the GameBoard becomes an observed value only through a named reading, and the derivation's targets are those transformations. The first dictionary, completed by the derivation mathematician as the targets close:

| The observed value | On the GameBoard | The reading that produces it |
| --- | --- | --- |
| a distance | Links along a digital line | a row's round-trip flight time (a light clock as a ruler) or the fan's angle (a parallax); never the Link count itself |
| a time | intervals | a body's own clock: its turn count (its proper time) and its owed count (section 9) |
| a speed | Links per interval (a body's drive, a row's pace) | what a moving reader counts, Doppler 1 +- v / c through the crossing rule (target 2), or a flight time between two detectors |
| a mass | the content M | the push a body reads (target 3), the clock's slowing near it (series E) and the mass a detector reads of a bound body (binding) |
| an energy or a frequency | the phase rate n / d, the cost h | E = h f, the cost per phase step; the click's amount |
| a force | the push per interval, the coupling **C a** | the momentum change a body's drive shows (the orbit's closure, series D) |
| an angle or a direction | a direction of the fan F_P | the click's Node on a screen (the fan's cell), the pointer's phase |
| a probability | the Gram weight **f**^T **G f** | the click's frequency over records as the wheel sweeps (Born, 6.5) |
| c | 1 / sqrt 3 Links per interval | a ruler-and-clock reading between two detectors: a unit conversion (record 191) |

The consequences: an experiment produces its observable by a detector declared in the world file, never by reading the host's state (the register's pins are detector readings); the comparison of the law with nature goes through the transformation in both directions; a GameBoard quantity is never compared with nature directly.

**The road from code to formulas** (the owner, 2026-09-21, record 211): the chronology of how the work went from the run as the evidence to the derived number that a run confirms is [CODE_TO_FORMULAS.md](CODE_TO_FORMULAS.md), each step with its record.

**The three worlds** (the owner, 2026-09-21, record 212): every thing of the system defined in the vector world, the software world and our world, with the six transformations between them, is [THREE_WORLDS.md](THREE_WORLDS.md); a thing missing from that table is missing from the law's vocabulary.

**Decided and in build (2026-09-21):** the click without amplitudes (item 1b); the exact phase at the click; the golden wheel W = 4096; no tables and no registers at Nodes; the crossing rule; form B; the GameBoard as one vector map between events (the scheduler to the next carry, host-only, bit-identical); BEAM_LAW restated in the vector form once the three land (record 191). **The four gaps (the owner, 2026-09-21):** (1) the code's unification (the common motion, the exact phase at the click, the skip between events) still in build; (2) relativity: the law does not reproduce Lorentz's slowing and contraction, the documents show deviations; (3) the free parameters (masses, charges, couplings, resolutions) still fed in, one budget not shown to fix them; (4) validation against nature: detector readings against real experiments, one parameter set, no re-fitting. # 6. History, implementation and evidence

The Evidence and references tab in the central specification collects code anchors and scoped previous results. Immutable event and wave-origin history is distinct from a Node's bounded active references and does not authorize remote history reads. Cancellation must follow the selected branch causally without deleting past records, unrelated paths or spatial topology. Exact bounded lookup, stopping, conflicting-notice and future-packet handling still require closure. A return undoes a share of an event on the board; it does not rewrite the recorded history, and no event is moved. (Under the Beam Law, 2026-09-19, section 5.4: there is no return and no cancellation; the click is the only one-way border, and what a run records is in [the engine](ENGINE.md).)

Git documents and source code describe particular software interfaces and experimental profiles; each result applies only to its identified source revision and tested scope. Existing generic-contact or capture samplers do not by themselves satisfy the Detector-only rule of section 3.19. Older shared-clock profiles and an incomplete six-output-clock candidate do not establish the complete adopted engine contract. Candidate annexes support implementation review but cannot silently replace adopted definitions. Bell results recorded with the former shared resource (section 3.18, deleted) are historical and are not evidence for the current model, and PASS/RETURN values carried on returning rays do not by themselves establish entanglement or spacelike Bell correlations. This documentation review adds no simulator execution or new passing result.

# 7. Working method

Only developers write or modify runtime code and tests. The architect coordinates interfaces; physics and mathematics own meanings and operators; experimental review fixes independent expectations; documentation maintains definitions and evidence status. These roles do not imply continuously running agents. (The roles as they run since 2026-09-21 are in skills/workflow.md, "How the team works now", record 309 of LOG_2026-09-20.)

# 8. Documentation integrity check

The central specification owns adopted model decisions, the defined Detector and Node/Link acceptance, with open items identified explicitly. This document summarizes that owner rather than maintaining competing detailed laws. Review ordinary locality without exception, Detector-only sampling at marked Nodes, six-output timing and exact ownership. Distinguish unresolved numerical choices, engineering gaps and unproved emergence from already adopted principles. A candidate, unsupported profile or historical result must never silently override an adopted definition. (Since 2026-09-17 this file owns the adopted decisions, section 5.4; the central specification is the historical source up to the 2026-09-16 revision.)

Do not suggest a next step or follow-up action unless Alon explicitly asks for it.
